Result: 在相机位姿估计、视频深度估计和三维重建上均取得一致提升，显著缓解长序列评估中的性能退化；在 KITTI Odometry 上，平均 ATE 比 LongStream 低 1.68 倍。

Conclusion: Info3R 通过在测试时进行信息自适应的状态更新与重置，提升了在线三维重建在长序列（尤其是户外长序列）上的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Transformer-based models have recently achieved strong performance on 3D reconstruction from images, and recent works extend them to process video streams in an online manner for real-world deployment. However, existing methods overlook two key signals when handling long image streams: the importance of each incoming frame and the information saturation of the model's internal state. In this paper, we propose Info3R, a novel information-adaptive test-time training method for the online 3D reconstruction. We introduce an information-aware state update that modulates the state update strength based on the redundancy and informativeness of each incoming frame. To restore the state's plasticity -- its capacity to incorporate new observations -- we propose a dynamic state reset, triggered by the cumulative magnitude of state updates and the model's prediction confidence and accompanied by an anchor-to-world alignment. Our method achieves consistent improvements on camera pose estimation, video depth estimation, and 3D reconstruction, while substantially mitigating the performance degradation in the long sequence evaluation. Notably, on KITTI Odometry, our method achieves on average 1.68x lower ATE than LongStream, demonstrating its robustness on extended outdoor sequences.

</details>


### [71] [PRIME: Perception Feedback with Situational Memory Embeddings in VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22040)
*Erik Deinzer,Naya Baslan,Luca Paparusso,Narunas Vaskevicius,Peter Knott,Luigi Palmieri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PRIME 提出一种学习的反馈机制，用情境记忆（Situational Memory）为 VLA 的感知查询注入下游意图，仅增加 0.41% 参数即在 Bench2Drive 闭环基准上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶 VLA 模型以「感知—推理—规划」的前馈方式运行。尽管现代架构在感知模块内部保留了时间递归，但早期感知对下游推理与导航目标「视而不见」，处理视觉输入时不加区分，无法根据先前决策来优先关注相关线索，造成感知与意图之间的脱节。

Method: 提出 PRIME——一种学习式反馈机制，使 VLA 的感知查询以新颖的「情境记忆」为条件。该情境记忆通过交叉注意力聚合 L 步窗口内过去感知、推理、导航目标与预测行为的潜在表示，从而实现意图驱动的感知注意力。方法计算开销极小，在 7.3B 参数基座模型上最多仅增加 29.7M 参数（占 0.41%）。

Result: 在 Bench2Drive 闭环基准上，PRIME 取得 SOTA 驾驶分数 82.47（较 ORION 提升 +4.73），成功率 60.00%（提升 +5.38 个百分点），为基于 Think2Drive 示范训练且已发表的 VLA 中报告的最高驾驶分数。

Conclusion: 通过将感知查询建立在包含历史感知、推理、目标与行为的情境记忆之上，PRIME 以极低的参数代价实现了自顶向下的意图驱动感知，显著提升闭环自动驾驶性能，验证了将下游反馈引入早期感知的有效性。

Abstract: Current Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models for autonomous driving operate primarily through feedforward inference across the perception--reasoning--planning hierarchy. While modern architectures maintain temporal recurrence within the perceptual module, early perception remains blind to downstream reasoning and navigation goals, processing visual inputs agnostically without prioritizing cues informed by prior decisions. To bridge this gap, this paper introduces PRIME, a learned feedback mechanism that conditions the VLA perceptual queries on a novel Situational Memory. By aggregating latent representations of past perception, reasoning, navigation goals, and predicted behaviors across an L-step window via cross-attention, PRIME enables intent-driven perceptual attention at minimal computational cost, adding only a maximum of 29.7M parameters (0.41% of the 7.3B-parameter base model). Evaluated on the Bench2Drive closed-loop benchmark, PRIME achieves a state-of-the-art Driving Score of 82.47 (+4.73 over ORION) and a Success Rate of 60.00% (+5.38 percentage points), the highest reported Driving Score among published VLAs trained on Think2Drive demonstrations.

</details>


### [72] [Traffic Sign Recognition for Autonomous Driving Using Branched YOLOv2 and Geometric Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22060)
*Arefeh Rezaei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于YOLOv2的交通标志识别系统，通过分支网络结构实现早期退出以加快推理，并引入无监督贝叶斯分割得到的几何模板信息以降低相似标志间的分类错误，自建GTSDB+GTSRB数据集上mAP最高提升至0.713。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通标志识别需同时高效地定位标志并判定其语义类别，是自动驾驶与高级驾驶辅助系统的关键感知任务。现有方法在计算效率与相似标志区分上仍有不足，因此需要既能减少推理耗时、又能降低视觉相似标志误分类的技术方案。

Method: 以YOLOv2为基线实现检测与分类一体化。第一，加入中间预测层形成分支架构，支持简单样本提前终止推理，并研究整图分支与单元格分支两种策略。第二，引入几何信息：用无监督贝叶斯图像分割在YOLOv2边界框内生成二值表示，与类别专属几何模板比对，该信息可用于推理阶段或作为训练辅助信号。数据方面，通过无缝克隆与受控图像变换融合GTSDB与GTSRB构建专用数据集，涵盖10类交通标志，训练3000、测试300样本。

Result: 所选分支架构运行时间0.647 s、mAP 0.680；基线YOLOv2为0.6607 s、mAP 0.680。推理阶段几何验证将mAP提升至0.713；几何特征训练变体达到mAP 0.697，运行时间0.6608 s。

Conclusion: 分支架构可在保持mAP不变的情况下缩短推理时间；几何模板验证是提升分类精度、尤其区分视觉相似交通标志的有效手段，其中推理期几何验证的精度收益最为显著。

Abstract: Traffic sign recognition (TSR) is an important perception task for autonomous driving and advanced driver-assistance systems, where a system must both localize traffic signs and determine their semantic classes efficiently. This work presents a TSR system based on YOLOv2 for simultaneous detection and classification. Two complementary modifications are studied. First, YOLOv2 is extended with intermediate prediction layers, forming a branched architecture that can terminate inference early for easy cases and reduce computation time. Both whole-image and cell-wise branching strategies are investigated. Second, geometric information is introduced to reduce classification errors between visually similar signs. An unsupervised Bayesian image-segmentation method produces binary representations that are compared with class-specific geometric templates inside YOLOv2 bounding boxes. This information is used either during inference or as an additional signal during training. A dedicated dataset is constructed by combining GTSDB and GTSRB samples using seamless cloning and controlled image transformations. Experiments cover ten traffic-sign classes, with 3,000 training and 300 test samples. The selected branched architecture reports 0.647 s runtime and 0.680 mAP, compared with 0.6607 s and 0.680 mAP for baseline YOLOv2. Geometric verification during inference increases mAP to 0.713, while the geometric-feature training variant achieves 0.697 mAP with a reported runtime of 0.6608 s.

</details>


### [73] [OmniVBench: A Benchmark and Large-Scale Dataset for Omni Reference-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22069)
*Wenxue Li,Peiyan Guan,Haoyang Jiang,Junxian Cai,Hualuo Liu,Chunjie Zhang,Chong Guan,Songlian Li,Taiyi Wu,Yongjian Yu,Xiaotong Zhao,Alan Zhao,Eric Liu,Xi Chen,Yu Liu,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 OmniVBench 评测基准与 Omni-R2V Dataset 训练数据集，面向全能参考到视频（omni R2V）生成，覆盖 7 类任务族、18 项细粒度任务，并采用因子锚定评估；同时提供 34 万条工业级训练样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参考到视频（R2V）生成正朝向通用化、多能力的方向发展，形成 omni R2V 新范式。但现有基准测试存在两方面不足：一是测试用例覆盖的参考类型与组合有限；二是评测协议多停留在整体参考一致性层面，未考察参考因子是否被正确保留、解耦与路由。此外，omni R2V 训练数据构建成本高昂，导致可用训练资源稀缺。

Method: 提出 OmniVBench 基准：扩展参考类型、细粒度控制任务与更丰富的参考组合，涵盖内容、运动、风格、结构、叙事及多参考等 7 类任务族、18 项细粒度任务；引入因子锚定评估，包含 12,172 条针对单个用例的检查清单项，用于判断目标参考因子是否被忠实保留、正确解耦并绑定至对应目标、以及是否按指令正确实现。同时构建 Omni-R2V Dataset：主要基于大规模专业视频素材语料，经处理得到 34 万条训练样本，覆盖多样参考类型与多参考组合，并设计任务专用流水线完成参考—目标配对构建。

Result: 对先进的开源与闭源 R2V 模型进行的大量评测显示，在 OmniVBench 上各任务族与各评估维度均存在明显性能差距，暴露出当前 R2V 模型的局限。

Conclusion: OmniVBench 与 Omni-R2V Dataset 为 omni R2V 模型的评测与训练提供了更全面的基准与可扩展的数据构建方案，揭示了现有模型在多类参考控制任务上的不足。

Abstract: Reference-to-video (R2V) generation is evolving toward increasingly general and versatile reference control, giving rise to the emerging paradigm of omni R2V generation. However, existing benchmarks fall short of these emerging capabilities: their test cases cover limited reference types and compositions, and their evaluation protocols largely assess holistic reference consistency, overlooking whether reference factors are properly preserved, disentangled, and routed. Meanwhile, the high cost of constructing omni R2V training data makes suitable training resources scarce. To address these gaps, we introduce OmniVBench and the Omni-R2V Dataset for evaluating and training omni R2V models. OmniVBench expands R2V evaluation across broader reference types, fine-grained control tasks, and richer reference compositions, covering 7 task families and 18 fine-grained tasks spanning content, motion, style, structure, narrative, and multi-reference settings. We introduce factor-grounded evaluation with 12,172 case-specific checklist items, assessing whether intended reference factors are faithfully preserved, correctly disentangled and bound to their targets, and properly realized according to the instruction. We further introduce the Omni-R2V Dataset, bringing industrial-grade training resources for diverse R2V tasks to the broader research community. Drawing primarily on a large-scale corpus of professional video footage, it comprises 340K processed training samples spanning diverse reference types and multi-reference compositions. We develop task-specific pipelines for reference-target pair construction, offering a practical and scalable recipe for omni R2V data construction. Extensive evaluation of advanced open- and closed-source R2V models reveals clear performance gaps across task families and evaluation dimensions on OmniVBench, highlighting remaining limitations of current R2V models.

</details>


### [74] [MintAct: A Unified Visual Agent for Digital Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22083)
*Mingfei Gao,Rui Tian,Haiming Gang,Bohan Zhai,Le Zhang,Yuanzheng Gong,Di Feng,Ege Özsoy,Kaixin Ma,Vishwesh Kirthivasan,Oğuzhan Fatih Kar,Roman Bachmann,Anders Boesen Lindbo Larsen,Afshin Dehghan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MintAct 是一系列 2B/4B/8B 规模的视觉语言模型，统一了 UI grounding、跨移动/桌面/网页的多步导航与视觉工具使用，性能可比肩各领域专用模型，并在 OSWorld-Verified 上取得 48.9 的 SOTA 成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 UI 智能体能力通常由各领域专用模型分别实现，缺乏一个在多领域（移动、桌面、网页）上统一且性能不逊于专用模型的通用模型；同时跨领域训练面临环境异构、反馈噪声与离线策略漂移等工程与算法挑战。

Method: 作者设计了可扩展的环境与强化学习基础设施：环境侧托管数百个跨异构领域后端的并发实例，服务于轨迹数据采集与在线 RL；训练侧采用异步框架，显式控制跨领域训练分布，并在噪声环境反馈与 off-policy 漂移下保持稳定。基于精心的环境、数据与训练配方设计，训练了 2B、4B、8B 三档模型。

Result: MintAct 在同等模型规模下于广泛基准上达到 SOTA，其中 OSWorld-Verified 得分为 48.9；在各能力上均匹配对应的领域专用模型表现。

Conclusion: 统一的环境、数据与训练配方配合可扩展的异步 RL 基础设施，能够用一个通用视觉语言模型覆盖 UI grounding、多步导航与视觉工具使用，且不牺牲各领域性能，验证了统一训练路线的可行性与可扩展性。

Abstract: We present MintAct, a family of vision-language models that unifies UI grounding, multi-step navigation across mobile, desktop, and web, and visual tool use, trained at 2B, 4B, and 8B scales. Through careful design of our environments, data, and training recipes, MintAct models match the performance of per-domain specialists across all of these capabilities. To enable this, we develop a scalable environment and reinforcement learning (RL) infrastructure. On the environment side, we host hundreds of concurrent instances across heterogeneous per-domain backends, serving both trajectory data collection and online RL. To enable efficient and scalable RL training, an asynchronous framework keeps explicit control over the cross-domain training distribution and remains stable under noisy environment feedback and off-policy drift. Experimental results show that MintAct achieves state-of-the-art performance (48.9 on OSWorld-Verified) across a wide range of benchmarks at comparable model sizes.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [75] [Do small language models know what they don't know?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20824)
*Prashant Mudgal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨基于熵的置信信号能否提升3B以下小语言模型（SLM）的准确率。发现token级熵在SLM中近乎失效（91%组合中平均token熵接近零），而语义熵可恢复有效置信信号，据此将不确定查询路由至更大专家模型可提升准确率最高+50个百分点；跨家族路由（平均+22.0%）显著优于同家族路由（+6.8%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SLM可在消费级硬件上运行，但准确率有限。作者希望验证熵类不确定性信号能否在如此小规模模型上提供可靠的置信度，从而在不显著增加计算成本的前提下提升准确率。

Method: 在7组模型对与5个标准NLU基准上评估7种方法，包括token级熵早停、语义熵估计与不确定性感知路由（将不确定查询交由更大的专家模型处理），并对比同家族与跨家族路由。

Result: token级熵在SLM中基本失效：91%的数据集-模型组合中平均token熵接近零，与答案正确性无关。语义熵（多次采样、按语义聚类、度量分布不确定性）可恢复有效信号；基于语义熵的选择性路由准确率提升最高达+50个百分点，跨家族路由平均+22.0%，高于同家族路由的+6.8%。

Conclusion: 熵类方法在SLM中的价值不在于节省算力，而在于智能算力分配——把更多token花在最需要的地方。专家模型质量比架构兼容性更重要。

Abstract: We explore whether entropy-based confidence signals can be leveraged to improve the accuracy of Small Language Models (SLMs) with fewer than 3 billion parameters, running entirely on consumer hardware. We evaluate seven distinct approaches, including token-level entropy early stopping, semantic entropy estimation, and uncertainty-aware routing to larger expert models, across 7 model pairs and 5 standard NLU benchmarks. Our key finding is that token-level entropy is effectively blind in SLMs: in 91% of dataset-model combinations, mean token entropy is near zero regardless of answer correctness, rendering token-based confidence signals unusable at this scale. We demonstrate that semantic entropy, computed by generating multiple samples, clustering answers by meaning, and measuring distributional uncertainty, recovers a viable confidence signal. Using semantic entropy to selectively route uncertain queries to a larger expert model yields accuracy improvements of up to +50 percentage points. Notably, cross-family routing (e.g., SmolLM 360M to Phi-3.5-mini) averages +22.0% improvement compared to +6.8% for same-family routing, revealing that expert model quality matters more than architectural compatibility. Our results suggest that the value proposition for entropy-based methods in SLMs is not computational savings but intelligent compute allocation: spending more tokens where they matter most.

</details>


### [76] [HERMES: Contrast-Aware Knowledge Graph Reasoning from Clinical Notes for Patient Outcome Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20825)
*Gia-Bach Nguyen,Hoang-Ha Nguyen,Tuan-Cuong Vuong,Trang Mai Xuan,Duy Quoc Ngo,Tien-Cuong Nguyen,Huan Vu,Thien Van Luong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HERMES是一个基于图的框架，仅使用临床文本，通过LLM引导构建个性化知识图谱并结合对比逻辑建模与图注意力网络，在MIMIC-III/IV的死亡率与再入院预测中优于强文本基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床预测模型多依赖结构化电子健康记录（如时间序列、手术编码），而利用非结构化临床笔记的方法通常将其编码为扁平序列，可能丢失临床叙事中显式的关系与时间结构。

Method: 提出HERMES图框架，仅基于临床文本并保留临床关系。包含两个核心：一是通过大语言模型引导从临床笔记中抽取构建个性化知识图谱（KG），并采用对比逻辑建模显式捕捉时间动态、治疗失败及结局变化；二是用图注意力网络（GAT）在KG上进行图学习，合成患者表示。

Result: 在MIMIC-III与MIMIC-IV上的院内死亡率和30天再入院预测任务中，HERMES持续优于强文本基线。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，结合对比逻辑建模的显式关系建模可显著提升预测性能。

Abstract: Clinical predictive models often rely on structured Electronic Health Record data, such as time-series and procedure codes. While recent approaches have begun leveraging unstructured clinical notes, they typically encode them as flat sequences, which may lose explicit relational and temporal structure present in clinical narratives. In response, we propose HERMES, a graph-based framework that operates exclusively on clinical text while preserving clinical relationships. This approach builds on two key ideas. First, personalized Knowledge Graphs (KGs) are constructed through Large-Language-Model-guided extraction from clinical notes with Contrastive Logic Modeling that explicitly captures temporal dynamics and treatment failures and changes in outcomes. Second, a Graph Attention Network synthesizes patient representations through graph-based learning over the KGs. Experiments on MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV for in-hospital mortality and 30-day readmission prediction show that HERMES consistently outperforms strong text-only baselines. Our findings demonstrate that explicit relational modeling with Contrastive Logic Modeling significantly advances predictive performance.

</details>


### [77] [TALON: A Temporally Aware Longitudinal Framework for Radiology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20826)
*Nien-Tsyr Sun,Min-Chen Chen,Hui Nien Hung,Vincent S. Tseng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出TALON框架，通过双通道时间融合模块自适应整合变长病史，提升纵向放射报告生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有放射报告生成模型多基于单次检查或仅最近一次既往检查，难以进行准确的纵向对比并检测细微的间隔变化；近期方法虽引入多次既往检查，但通常以固定长度聚合病史，未在融合前显式建模各既往检查的依角色相关性。

Method: TALON（Temporally Aware LONgitudinal RRG）框架，核心为双通道时间融合模块（DCTFM）：通过互补的相似性通道与变化通道分别将当前检查与各次既往检查对比，以捕捉持续征象与间隔变化；设计通道特定注意力估计各既往检查的相关性，并借助可学习的既往特异门控自适应整合有信息量的纵向证据、抑制冗余。

Result: 在MIMIC-CXR上，TALON在多项临床效能与基于图的指标上优于当前最先进方法；当可用的既往检查更多时，其性能进一步提升。

Conclusion: TALON的DCTFM在建模纵向RRG方面具有优势，可处理比现有方法更长、更复杂的患者病史。

Abstract: Current radiology report generation (RRG) models usually produce descriptive reports based on a single examination or only the most recent prior examination, limiting their ability to perform accurate and meaningful longitudinal comparisons and detect subtle interval changes. Although recent approaches have begun to incorporate multiple prior examinations, they usually aggregate a fixed-length history without explicitly modeling the role-dependent relevance of each prior examination before fusion. To address this, we propose TALON, a Temporally Aware LONgitudinal RRG framework that adaptively integrates variable-length patient histories. The underlying Dual-Channel Temporal Fusion Module (DCTFM) compares the current examination with each prior examination through complementary similarity and change channels to capture persistent findings and interval changes, respectively. The specially designed channel-specific attention estimates the relevance of each prior examination, while a learned prior-specific gate adaptively integrates informative longitudinal evidence and suppresses redundancy. Experiments on MIMIC-CXR show that TALON outperforms the current state-of-the-art method on various clinical efficacy and graph-based metrics. When more prior examinations become available, TALON's performance on these metrics improves even further, emphasizing the strength of TALON's DCTFM in modeling longitudinal RRG across longer and more complex patient histories than existing approaches.

</details>


### [78] [From Discharge Notes to Patient Understanding: Persona-Grounded, Open-Ended Simulation of LLMs as Discharge Educators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20827)
*Won Seok Jang,Zonghai Yao,Hong Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 DischargeBench：以人格化虚拟病人模拟多轮出院教育对话，由 Education Monitor Agent 维持病人真实感，从对话质量、主题清单、理解度、事实一致性四维度评估 LLM，揭示跨疾病章节与病人人格的临床相关差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 出院教育是交互式教学任务，临床医生需根据患者的健康素养、记忆能力与个性调整出院方案；而现有 LLM 评估多针对静态任务或文书生成，无法在开放式对话中测量患者理解程度。

Method: 构建 DischargeBench 人格化模拟框架：候选 LLM 扮演教育者，与 Virtual Patient 进行多轮会话；Education Monitor Agent 在不修改教育者的前提下调控病人真实感，以保护评估信号。构建 MIMIC-IV-Ext-DischargeBench 数据集，含 477 个案例、覆盖 24 个 ICD 章节，并设置人格轴（性格、教育水平、健康素养、病史回忆）以支持分层分析。采用经医师标注对齐的 LLM-as-a-Judge，从 Conversation Quality、Topic Checklist、Comprehension、Factual Consistency 四轴打分。

Result: 在闭源与开源 LLM 上，总体得分掩盖了跨 ICD 章节与患者人格的临床相关差异；困难人格暴露了话题覆盖失败、理解缺口以及源答案一致性下降等问题。

Conclusion: 面向出院教育的 LLM 评估应以患者理解为核心，而非仅关注文本质量或答案准确性。

Abstract: Hospital discharge education is an interactive teaching task: a clinician adapts a discharge plan to a patient's literacy, recall, and personality. Existing LLM evaluations target static or artifact-generation tasks and do not measure patient understanding under open-ended dialogue. We introduce DischargeBench, a persona-grounded simulation in which a candidate LLM educator conducts a multi-turn session with a Virtual Patient, while an Education Monitor Agent regulates patient realism without modifying the educator, protecting the evaluation signal. We curate MIMIC-IV-Ext-DischargeBench, 477 cases over 24 ICD chapters with persona axes (personality, education level, health literacy, past-medical-history recall) for stratified analysis. Each simulation is scored on four axes -- Conversation Quality, Topic Checklist, Comprehension, and Factual Consistency -- by an LLM-as-a-Judge aligned against physician annotations. Across closed- and open-source LLMs, aggregate scores conceal clinically relevant variation across ICD chapters and patient personas; difficult personas expose coverage failures, comprehension gaps, and reduced source-answer agreement. LLM evaluation for discharge education should center patient understanding, not text quality or answer accuracy alone.

</details>


### [79] [Beyond WER: Entity and Disfluency Recall in Accented Conversational ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20828)
*Fiza Husain,Ankit Pandey,Yash Singh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 面向印度、印尼和拉美口音英语学习者，提出三阶段ASR流水线，在大幅提升命名实体与填充词召回的同时将WER控制在6-10%，以30亿参数模型达到300亿零样本模型水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以WER为优化目标的ASR系统在带口音的会话英语中常漏识命名实体和填充停顿，而这两者对语言学习反馈至关重要。现有系统（如Whisper、商用ASR）在实体召回上表现不佳，且大模型（30B）计算成本高。作者希望以更小参数量的模型同时兼顾实体召回、填充词召回与低WER。

Method: 三阶段流水线：(1) 启发式SQL过滤，构建实体密集训练数据，实体密度为随机采样的2.8倍；(2) 基于Qwen2.5-Omni-3B的LoRA区域适配器微调，单次前向即可输出逐字（verbatim）与纠正（corrected）两种转写；(3) 六类错误分类体系，由LLM裁判验证（与210个人工标注样本的一致率83.8%）。

Result: 在6千条测试语音上：实体召回从53-55%提升至80-85%，填充词召回从<5%提升至76-86%，WER为6-10%；在实体召回上优于Whisper和某商用ASR，并以少10倍的参数量匹配零样本30B模型。配对bootstrap检验显示，仅数据筛选一项即贡献2.8-4.2个百分点的实体召回提升（p<0.0001）。

Conclusion: 数据筛选、区域化LoRA适配与LLM可验证的错误分类相结合，可在小参数量模型上同时实现高实体/填充词召回与低WER，为口音英语学习场景提供高效可行的ASR方案。

Abstract: ASR systems optimised for Word Error Rate (WER) often miss named entities and filled pauses in accented conversational English, both critical for language-learning feedback. We present a three-stage pipeline for speakers from India, Indonesia, and Latin America: (1) heuristic SQL filters curating entity-rich training data at 2.8x the entity density of random sampling, (2) regional LoRA adapters fine-tuned on Qwen2.5-Omni-3B producing both verbatim and corrected transcripts in a single forward pass, and (3) a six-category error taxonomy validated by an LLM-based judge (83.8% agreement, 210 human-labelled samples). The pipeline achieves 80-85% entity recall (up from 53-55%), 76-86% filler recall (up from <5%), and 6-10% WER across 6k test utterances, outperforming Whisper and a commercial ASR on entity recall while matching a zero-shot 30B model with 10x fewer parameters. Paired bootstrap tests confirm that curation alone accounts for 2.8-4.2 pp of entity recall gain (p<0.0001).

</details>


### [80] [SAGE: Schema-Guided LLMs for Grant Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20829)
*Erik Varapaev,Andrei Chetvergov,Stepan Ukolov,Timofei Sivoraksha,Alexander Evseev,Sergey Bolovtsov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SAGE 是一项基于结构化评审标准的资助申请评估系统，将评审规则分解为可核查条目并关联申请材料中的证据。在35份非营利资助申请上开展两阶段评估：与原始105份评审的事后比较达到 kappa=0.29，经基金会标准层面复核（202份评估）后 kappa 升至0.58，并优于单提示基线（kappa=0.33）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 资助评审员需依据细致标准审阅申请表、预算与佐证材料，同时产出可供同行核查的评估结论。现有流程对结构化、可追溯和可审计性的支持不足，因此需要将评审方法学落地为可核查、证据链接的系统。

Method: 提出 SAGE（Schema-Guided Aspect-Based Grant Evaluation），将资助评审 rubric 转化为结构化检查项，并把系统判断与申请包中的证据相链接。在35份非营利资助申请上进行两阶段评估：先与原始竞赛的105份评审做事后比较；随后基金会在查看 SAGE 结果后进行标准层面复核，产生202份评估，并与单提示每标准基线对比。另进行 claim 级审计以识别结构化草稿中被确认、有争议和未涉及的部分。

Result: 事后比较：与原始105份评审达到中等程度的序数一致性（kappa=0.29）。辅助复核轮：kappa=0.58，优于单提示基线（共同子集 kappa=0.33），且秩相关更高、误差更低。claim 级审计可识别被确认、有争议和未涉及的内容。

Conclusion: SAGE 通过生成详细、证据链接且可审计的评审草稿，将评审方法学操作化，供专家修正使用；在辅助复核条件下可提升与专家评估的一致性，但事后独立比较的一致性仍有限。

Abstract: Grant reviewers must apply detailed criteria to application forms, budgets, and supporting documents while producing assessments that colleagues can inspect. We present SAGE, Schema-Guided Aspect-Based Grant Evaluation, a system that translates a grant rubric into structured checks and links its judgements to evidence from the application package. We evaluate SAGE in two stages on 35 nonprofit grant applications. A post-factum comparison with 105 reviews from the original competition shows fair ordinal agreement (kappa = 0.29). The foundation then conducted a criterion-level re-review after inspecting SAGE, producing 202 assessments. In this assisted round, SAGE reached kappa = 0.58 and outperformed a one-prompt-per-criterion baseline (kappa = 0.33 on the common subset), with higher rank correlation and lower error. A claim-level audit further identifies confirmed, disputed, and unaddressed parts of the structured draft. SAGE operationalizes the review methodology by producing a detailed, evidence-linked, and auditable draft for expert correction.

</details>


### [81] [Reviser: Revision-Capable Text Generation via Autoregressive Cursor Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20830)
*Sean Diab*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Reviser，一种仅在可变画布上生成“光标相对动作”序列的 decoder-only Transformer，实现真正的非单调文本生成，同时保持简单的下一动作预测接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具备修订能力的生成很有吸引力，因为它能插入或修改较早的内容；但许多非自回归与基于编辑的方法需要通过重复的序列级计算才能获得这种灵活性，代价较高。

Method: Reviser 在可变画布上逐步生成响应：每一步仅预测一个动作 token，即 INSERT(token)、MOVE(Δ) 或 STOP；模型在“编辑历史动作”上自回归，而非按最终文本顺序自回归。

Result: 在续写基准上，arena 评估中 Reviser 显著优于 SEDD 和 MDLM；轨迹统计表明模型频繁执行向后移动与画布中部插入，而非仅模拟末端追加解码。与同规模自回归基线相比，在 100M 和 300M 规模下均具竞争力；在统一 FLOPs 约定下，推理计算量显著少于代表性的多轮精修与扩散式基线。

Conclusion: Reviser 以简洁的下一动作接口实现非单调生成，兼顾生成质量与推理效率，为可修订生成提供了有竞争力的方案。

Abstract: Revision-capable generation is appealing because it can insert or revise earlier content, but many non-autoregressive and edit-based approaches obtain this flexibility through repeated sequence-level computation. We propose Reviser, a decoder-only Transformer that generates a response as a sequence of cursor-relative actions on a mutable canvas. At each step, Reviser predicts exactly one action token: INSERT(token), MOVE($Δ$), or STOP, and is autoregressive over edit-history actions rather than final text order. This design enables genuinely non-monotonic generation while preserving a simple next-action interface. On a continuation benchmark, Reviser is strongly preferred to SEDD and MDLM in our arena evaluations, and trajectory statistics confirm that the model performs frequent backward moves and mid-canvas insertions rather than merely emulating end-append decoding. Against size-matched autoregressive baselines, Reviser is competitive at both the 100M and 300M scales. Under our shared FLOPs convention, Reviser also requires substantially less inference compute than representative multi-pass refinement and diffusion-style baselines.

</details>


### [82] [Recursive Language Models Generalize Out of Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20831)
*Chenxiao Yang,Zhiyuan Li,David McAllester,Nathan Srebro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 将语言模型每步推理限制在隔离子任务上下文中（递归式）能提升域外学习，因为标准思维链虽能表达递归规则，但简单性偏好会驱使其利用跨子任务的上下文捷径，一旦token变化即失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究"限制语言模型可见范围"何时能改善学习，比较标准思维链与递归语言模型：前者读取完整轨迹，后者将每个子任务隔离求解。核心动机在于探讨通用性与域外泛化之间的权衡，以及为何覆盖正确规则并不足以实现真正的推理。

Method: 理论分析对比标准CoT（更通用的学习器，读取完整推理轨迹）与递归语言模型（通过隔离子任务上下文进行自我限制求解）。在分布内分析IID泛化保证的变化；在分布外分析CoT依赖子任务外上下文形成捷径的失效模式及递归上下文隔离的作用。

Result: 分布内：CoT能高效模拟递归规则，IID泛化保证仅变化常数因子，递归并无明显优势。分布外：CoT可通过依赖当前子任务之外的上下文来拟合训练数据，形成捷径，当这些token变化时失效；递归的上下文隔离排除了这种失败模式。尽管CoT的假设类仍覆盖递归规则，但简单性偏好选择了捷径而非真实规则。

Conclusion: 要超越分布准确率并真正推理，仅覆盖正确规则是不够的，这与经典学习理论形成对比；上下文隔离通过消除捷径来提升域外学习。

Abstract: We study when limiting what a language model can see improves learning. We compare standard CoT, the more general learner that reads the full trace, with recursive language models, which restricts itself by solving each subtask in an isolated context. In-distribution, this generality comes for free: CoT can efficiently simulate the recursive rule, so the IID generalization guarantee changes only by a constant factor, and recursion does not offer much. But out of domain, CoT can fit training by relying on context outside the current subtask, i.e. a shortcut that breaks once those tokens change; recursive context isolation rules out this failure mode. Even though CoT's class still covers the recursive rule, simplicity bias picks the shortcut over the truth. Thus, to go beyond distributional accuracy and truly reason, covering the right rule is not enough; this contrasts with classical learning theory.

</details>


### [83] [TatBLiMP: A Benchmark of Linguistic Minimal Pairs for Tatar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20832)
*Ilshat Saetov,Dmitry Gaynullin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TatBLiMP是首个针对鞑靼语的语法最小对立对基准，覆盖16种形态句法现象、1248对句子，仅比较单语素差异并依赖模型已分配的概率评分，无需生成或句法分析器；实验显示其更反映鞑靼语专项训练而非参数量规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语法评测资源缺乏对鞑靼语（西里尔字母书写的钦察突厥语）的支持，甚至连覆盖101种语言的MultiBLiMP也未纳入鞑靼语，因此此前不存在任何针对鞑靼语模型的语法性评测；作者希望填补这一空白。

Method: 构建TatBLiMP：沿用TurBLiMP的现象清单与单语素扰动操作，并新增鞑靼语特有的“数词/量词后裸名词数”现象；每对句子仅差一个语素，合法句取自鞑靼语文学散文的真实句子，非法句由apertium-tat转换器确定性生成，全部经母语者审核，并遵循合理性原则使非法句为真实可能出现的错误。评分上直接比较模型对合法/非法成员的概率，不生成文本、不需句法分析器，可运行于基座模型与中期训练检查点。

Result: 在从零训练的鞑靼语模型、跨语言适配模型及前沿多语言大模型上评测：478M从零模型与125M单语模型接近0.97领先；7B适配模型落后；30–120B前沿模型为0.80–0.92；轻度调优的多语言模型最弱。基准性能与鞑靼语专项训练相关，而非参数规模。

Conclusion: TatBLiMP是首个鞑靼语语法评测基准，可有效区分专项训练效果。其主要局限在于继承的分类体系遗漏了母语者最敏感的形态音系现象（元音和谐与辅音同化），作者提出一个本土化第二层评测以补充这些现象。

Abstract: We introduce TatBLiMP, the first benchmark of linguistic minimal pairs for Tatar (tt, ISO 639-3 tat), a Qypchaq Turkic language written in Cyrillic. To our knowledge it is the first grammaticality evaluation for Tatar language models of any kind, since even the 101-language MultiBLiMP does not include Tatar. TatBLiMP covers 16 morphosyntactic phenomena in 1248 sentence pairs. Each pair differs by a single morpheme, one grammatical and one ungrammatical. A model passes a pair when it assigns higher probability to the grammatical member. Scoring compares probabilities the model already assigns, so the benchmark needs no text generation and no parser, and it runs on base models and on mid-training checkpoints. TatBLiMP adapts the phenomenon inventory and single-morpheme breaking operations of TurBLiMP to Tatar and adds one phenomenon specific to Tatar, bare-noun number after numerals and quantifiers. The grammatical member of every pair is an attested sentence from Tatar literary prose. The ungrammatical member is produced by a deterministic single-morpheme perturbation with the apertium-tat transducer. Every pair is ratified by a native speaker. A plausibility principle governs construction, so the ungrammatical member is a plausible real-world error rather than an arbitrary corruption. Across from-scratch Tatar models, cross-lingual adaptations, and frontier multilingual LLMs, the benchmark tracks focused Tatar training rather than parameter scale. A 478M from-scratch model and a 125M monolingual model lead near 0.97, a 7B adaptation trails, frontier LLMs of 30-120B parameters fall to 0.80-0.92, and a lightly tuned multilingual model is weakest. We close with the benchmark's main limitation. Its inherited taxonomy omits the morphophonology, vowel harmony and consonant assimilation, that is most salient to native speakers, and we sketch a native second layer that would add it.

</details>


### [84] [Transsion's Speaker-Attributed Multilingual ASR System for the MLC-SLM 2026 Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20833)
*Zhecheng Ren,Xuanji He,Xiaoxiao Li,Zhichen Han,Gaoyang Dong,Gaosheng Zhang,Minchuan Chen,Fengjie Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 Transsion 语音团队在 MLC-SLM 2026 挑战赛 Task 1（多语种对话语音的说话人归属转写）中的提交系统：采用“说话人日志 + 长音频多语种 ASR + 说话人-转写融合”的级联框架，在官方评测集上取得 15.41% 的 tcpMER，位列参赛队伍第二名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向多语种对话语音的说话人归属转写任务，需要同时解决“谁在何时说话”（说话人日志）与“说了什么”（多语种识别）两个子问题，并将二者精确对齐。现有单一模型难以兼顾长音频、多语种与时间戳精度，因此需要构建模块化级联系统以整合各环节优势。

Method: 提出三组件级联框架：(1) 说话人日志模块基于 DiariZen，通过局部说话人活动估计与全局说话人聚类生成说话人同质分段；(2) 长音频多语种 ASR 模块基于 Qwen3-Omni 生成多语种转写，并借助外部 CTC 对齐模型提供词级与字符级精确时间戳；(3) 融合模块将日志输出与带时间戳的转写结果结合，生成带说话人归属的 STM 输出。

Result: 在官方评测集上的实验验证了该框架的有效性，提交系统取得 15.41% 的 tcpMER，在全部参赛队伍中排名第二。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，基于 DiariZen 说话人日志、Qwen3-Omni 多语种 ASR 与 CTC 时间戳对齐的级联融合方案，能够有效完成多语种对话语音的说话人归属转写，并在挑战赛中获得具有竞争力的性能。

Abstract: This paper presents the Transsion Speech Team submission to Task 1 of the MLC-SLM 2026 Challenge, which focuses on speaker-attributed transcription for multilingual conversational speech. We propose a cascaded framework consisting of three components: a speaker diarization module, a long-form multilingual ASR module, and a speaker-transcription fusion module. The diarization module is built upon DiariZen and produces speaker-homogeneous segments through local speaker activity estimation and global speaker clustering. The ASR module is based on Qwen3-Omni and generates multilingual transcriptions, while an external CTC-based alignment model provides precise word- and character-level timestamps. Finally, the fusion module combines diarization outputs with timestamped transcriptions to generate speaker-attributed STM outputs. Experimental results on the official evaluation set demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework. The submitted system achieves a tcpMER of 15.41% and ranks second among all participating teams.

</details>


### [85] [Towards Secure Cloud-Native Computing: Unveiling Kubernetes Misconfigurations with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20834)
*Mostafa Anouar Ghorab,Mohamed Aymen Saied*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨利用大语言模型(LLM)识别Kubernetes配置错误，提出配置错误分类体系，实证评估现有检测工具，并分析易出错对象及其严重性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云原生生态系统中Kubernetes已成为容器编排的事实标准，但其复杂性导致配置错误频发，威胁安全与性能，亟需更有效的检测方法。

Method: 构建常见配置错误类型的分类体系；对现有最先进检测工具进行实证评估与基准对比；分析最易发生配置错误的Kubernetes对象并评估问题严重性；引入LLM等先进机器学习技术。

Result: 通过基准评估揭示了现有检测工具的有效性，识别出易发生配置错误的Kubernetes对象类别，并评估了所发现问题的严重程度。

Conclusion: 借助LLM等先进机器学习技术，为改进配置错误检测方法提供了新见解，有助于提升云原生环境的安全性与性能。

Abstract: In the rapidly evolving landscape of cloud-native computing, Organizations are increasingly adopting infrastructure models that emphasize scalability, flexibility, and efficiency. Kubernetes has become the de facto standard for orchestrating containerized applications in these environments. However, the inherent complexity of cloud-native ecosystems introduces significant challenges, particularly in the form of misconfigurations that can compromise both security and performance. This study explores the potential of Large Language Models (LLMs) in identifying Kubernetes misconfigurations. We introduce a comprehensive taxonomy of common misconfiguration types, offering a structured framework to better understand and categorize these issues. Additionally, we conduct an empirical evaluation of state-of-the-art detection tools to benchmark their effectiveness. Furthermore, we analyze the Kubernetes objects most prone to misconfiguration and evaluate the severity of the identified issues. By leveraging advanced machine learning techniques, including LLMs, we provide novel insights into enhancing misconfiguration detection methodologies.

</details>


### [86] [A Generative Grammar Underlying the Voynich Manuscript, the Pastiche Hypothesis: Evidence from Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20835)
*Nicolas Turenne*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究对十五世纪伏尼契手稿进行跨学科分析，发现其符号呈字母特征、语音风格偏闪米特语系、统计上模仿自然语言，且植物插图与地中海传统药草书相符，支持其为一套结合语言规律与草药知识的结构化生成体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伏尼契手稿使用未知文字且内容至今未被破译。既有研究显示其统计特性接近自然语言，插图（多为植物）又令人联想到中世纪药草书，这一矛盾促使作者以跨学科方法进一步检验其书写系统的性质与来源。

Method: 作者基于新转写语料，融合概率建模、语音分解、稀有事件检测与多模态图像分析。用位置依赖的概率文法刻画词级与字母级分布；将语音模式与印欧语、闪米特语及亚洲语言比较；并采用基于大语言模型的图文对齐方法识别潜在的植物学对应关系。

Result: 结果显示，手稿符号的行为更像字母而非音节单位，但词长分布却类似音节结构。语音分析表明其与希伯来语、阿拉伯语等辅音比重高的语言更接近，而非印欧语。概率建模可重现类齐普夫分布，并发现重复首字母序列概率极低，说明是对自然语言的结构化模仿。图像分析显示手稿植物插图与地中海传统的伪阿普列乌斯药草书插图高度吻合，符合对中世纪医书的模仿。

Conclusion: 这些发现支持如下假设：伏尼契手稿遵循一套结合语言规律与草药知识的结构化生成系统；同时表明在历史手稿分析中整合概率方法与人工智能辅助手段具有重要价值。

Abstract: Background: The Voynich Manuscript is a fifteenth-century codex written in an unknown script whose content remains undeciphered. Previous studies suggest that its statistical properties resemble those of natural languages, while its illustrations - primarily plants - recall medieval herbals.
  Methods: We present a multidisciplinary analysis combining probabilistic modeling, phonetic decomposition, rare-event detection, and multimodal image analysis, based on a newly transliterated corpus. Word- and letter-level distributions are modeled using position-dependent probabilistic grammars, while phonetic patterns are compared across Indo-European, Semitic, and Asian languages. Image-text alignment methods based on large language models are applied to identify potential botanical correspondences.
  Results: The results indicate that Voynich symbols behave as letters rather than syllabic units, while word-length distributions resemble syllabic structures. Phonetic analyses show closer alignment with consonant-heavy languages such as Hebrew or Arabic than with Indo-European languages. Probabilistic modeling reproduces Zipf-like distributions and reveals extremely low probabilities for repeated initial-letter sequences, indicating a structured imitation of natural language. Image analysis suggests strong correspondences between Voynich plant illustrations and those found in Pseudo-Apuleius herbals from the Mediterranean tradition, consistent with an imitation of medieval medicinal books.
  Perspectives: These findings support the hypothesis that the Voynich Manuscript follows a structured generative system combining linguistic regularities and herbal knowledge, and demonstrate the value of integrating probabilistic and AI-assisted approaches in the analysis of historical manuscripts.

</details>


### [87] [PhysioBench: A Unified Benchmark for Physiological Signal Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20836)
*Mengxuan Li,Junfa Chen,Jinze Xia,Yundan Chen,Lixin Fan,Ke Liu,Keyue Shi,Haishuai Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PhysioBench，一个统一的生理信号问答基准，整合22个公开数据集为6140万个问题、覆盖30个任务，并评估21个代表性模型，发现现有模型尚无法在各生理信号模态与任务上保持一致优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生理信号基础模型通常需要针对每个任务做特定适配；自然语言虽可作为统一接口来指定不同预测目标，但当前模型在跨生理信号模态遵循此类指令的能力仍缺乏充分评估。

Method: 构建PhysioBench统一基准：将22个公开数据集的标注协调统一为6140万个问答对，覆盖30个任务，每个问答对均基于信号片段并溯源至原始标注；在三种互补设置下评估21个代表性模型，包括大语言模型、视觉-语言模型、时间序列语言模型及生理信号基础模型。

Result: 没有任何被评估模型能在所有生理信号模态与任务上保持一致强性能；引入自然语言可支持跨任务统一预测，但性能对问题表述方式仍然敏感。

Conclusion: PhysioBench为生理信号理解的细粒度分析和未来研究提供了可扩展平台，代码已开源。

Abstract: Physiological signals support diverse clinical and monitoring tasks, yet existing physiological signal foundation models typically require task-specific adaptation for each task. Natural language provides a common interface for specifying different prediction objectives, but the ability of current models to follow such instructions across physiological signal modalities remains insufficiently evaluated. To address this gap, we introduce PhysioBench, a unified benchmark for physiological signal question answering. PhysioBench harmonizes annotations from 22 public datasets into 61.4 million questions across 30 tasks. Each question-answer pair is grounded in a signal segment and traceable to its source annotation. We evaluate 21 representative models, including large language models, vision-language models, time-series language models, and physiological signal foundation models under three complementary settings. The results show that none of the evaluated models achieves consistently strong performance across physiological signal modalities and tasks. The incorporation of natural language supports unified prediction across tasks, although performance remains sensitive to question formulation. Beyond these findings, PhysioBench offers an extensible platform for fine-grained analysis and future research on physiological signal understanding. Our codes are available at https://github.com/Leanna97/PhysioBench.

</details>


### [88] [From Generation to Detection: Exploration of Discourse Driven Scenario based LLM Generated Fake News](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20838)
*Zeynep Özdemir,Murat Osmanoğlu,Sevgi Yiğit-Sert,Ömer Özgür Tanrıöver,Yılmaz Ar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究构建含14000篇合成假新闻的语料库，跨四种操纵场景评估主流LLM的假新闻生成与检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究现代LLM在不同操纵场景下生成与检测假新闻的表现，并评估生成策略对可检测性的影响。

Method: 使用七种主流模型，在开放式生成、改写、操纵提示和基于属性提示（依托新闻话语框架）四种场景下生成14000篇合成假新闻；分析其语言属性以评估与真实新闻的结构和语义相似度；再让各模型在基础检测提示与经迭代优化（从真假配对中提取误导模式）的改进提示下检测假新闻。

Result: 各模型在生成与检测虚假信息方面存在显著差异；生成策略强烈影响可检测性；优化后的提示未能提升检测性能，甚至常常产生负面影响。

Conclusion: 本研究系统评估了LLM在典型生成场景下检测LLM生成假新闻的能力。

Abstract: In this study, we examine how modern LLMs generate and detect fake news under controlled settings across four manipulation scenarios. These are open-ended generation, rewriting, manipulation prompts and attribute based prompts grounded in the journalistic discourse framework. Firstly, using seven widely adapted models, we created a synthetic fake news corpus with 14000 generated articles across these four scenarios. Then we analyzed its linguistic properties to assess how closely model-generated news resembles real news structurally and semantically. Finally, to evaluate detection performance, we conducted experiments where each model judges generated fake news, starting with a basic detection prompt and improved prompts developed through an iterative refinement process that extracts misleading patterns from real-fake pairs. Our results revealed substantial variation across models in both generating and detecting misinformation, demonstrated that the generation strategy strongly influences detectability, and show that the refined prompt does not improve and often harms detection performance. Therefore, the study provides a systematic assessment of LLMs detection capability of LLMs generated fake news across typical generation scenarios.

</details>


### [89] [Curriculum-Based Noise Adaptation for Phoneme-to-Text Reconstruction in Visual Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20839)
*Matthew Kit Khinn Teng,Haibo Zhang,Takeshi Saitoh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出渐进式错误课程训练（PECT），通过合成扰动、多域伪标签和目标域伪标签逐步适配NLLB音素到文本重建模型，缓解训练与推理间音素预测错误的失配，在LRS2/LRS3上提升多种视觉语音识别前端性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音素中心的视觉语音识别依赖中间音素预测重建句子，性能高度依赖音素到文本重建模型的鲁棒性。现有重建方法多在干净音素序列或人工合成扰动上训练，与推理时真实音素预测错误存在失配，限制了鲁棒性。

Method: 提出渐进式错误课程训练（PECT）。该课程学习框架基于NLLB的音素到文本重建模型，按由易到难的顺序引入合成音素扰动、多域伪标签、以及由视觉语音识别器生成的目标域伪标签，逐步让模型接触越来越真实的音素预测错误。

Result: 在LRS2和LRS3基准上，PECT在V-ASR、PV-ASR、HP-VSR等多种音素前端下均稳定提升重建性能。HP-VSR-FiLMFuse（L4）在LRS2上WER由23.3%降至22.2%；HP-VSR-ResFiLM在LRS3上WER由30.3%降至29.7%。消融实验与定性分析进一步验证了渐进适配的有效性。

Conclusion: PECT为音素中心视觉语音识别中的音素到文本重建提供了一种有效且可泛化的课程学习策略。

Abstract: Phoneme-centric visual speech recognition reconstructs sentences from intermediate phoneme predictions, making overall recognition performance highly dependent on the robustness of the phoneme-to-text reconstruction model. Existing reconstruction approaches are commonly trained on clean phoneme sequences or synthetically corrupted inputs, leading to a mismatch between training conditions and the realistic phoneme prediction errors encountered during inference. To address this limitation, this paper proposes progressive error curriculum training (PECT). This curriculum learning framework progressively adapts a No Language Left Behind (NLLB)-based phoneme-to-text reconstruction model using synthetic phoneme perturbations, multi-domain pseudo-labels, and target-domain pseudo-labels generated by a visual speech recognizer. By gradually exposing the reconstruction model to increasingly realistic phoneme prediction errors, the proposed framework improves robustness while preserving sentence-reconstruction accuracy. Experiments on the LRS2 and LRS3 benchmarks demonstrate that PECT consistently improves reconstruction performance across multiple phoneme-based visual speech recognition frontends, including visual automatic speech recognition (V-ASR), point visual automatic speech recognition (PV-ASR), and head-pose-aware visual speech recognition (HP-VSR) variants. In particular, PECT reduces the word error rate (WER) of HP-VSR-FiLMFuse (L4) from 23.3% to 22.2% on LRS2 and reduces the WER of HP-VSR-ResFiLM from 30.3% to 29.7% on LRS3. Comprehensive ablation studies and qualitative analyses further demonstrate the effectiveness of progressively adapting the reconstruction model to realistic phoneme prediction errors. These results show that PECT provides an effective and generalizable curriculum learning strategy for phoneme-to-text reconstruction in phoneme-centric visual speech recognition.

</details>


### [90] [COAL-SQL: Coverage-Guided Augmentation and Failure-Driven Learning for Text-to-SQL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20842)
*Qifeng Cai,Xuanguang Pan,Hao Liang,Chang Xu,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: COAL-SQL：结合覆盖率引导增强(CGA)与失败驱动学习(FDL)的统一框架，用12,600条样本在BIRD开发集达到64.9%执行准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源大模型进行复杂真实场景的Text-to-SQL生成仍需要任务特定的后训练，而后训练需要覆盖目标任务能力的数据与能让模型习得这些能力的学习策略。现有数据集对SQL结构覆盖不完整，增强方法只扩数据不识别结构缺口；且单独使用SFT或RL无法动态应对训练中暴露的弱点。

Method: 框架包含两部分：1) 覆盖率引导增强(CGA)：用贪心选择识别原数据集中缺失的SQL结构，构造互补样本以提升结构覆盖；2) 失败驱动学习(FDL)：以GRPO为主优化目标，同时为未解决样本提供定向监督——步级对强LLM生成的已验证推理轨迹累积失败做SFT，轮级基于累积失败检索结构相关样本构造定向练习。

Result: 仅用12,600条去重后训练样本，在BIRD开发集上取得64.9%执行准确率，优于同等规模训练的基线。

Conclusion: CGA与FDL结合的COAL-SQL能在有限数据规模下高效提升开源模型复杂SQL生成能力，数据效率与准确率均优于同规模基线。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL translates natural-language questions into executable SQL queries, but open-source large language models still require task-specific post-training for complex, real-world SQL generation. Effective post-training requires both training data that cover the capabilities demanded by the target task and a learning strategy that enables the model to acquire them. Existing datasets provide valuable supervision but incompletely cover SQL structures, while augmentation methods typically expand data without identifying structural gaps. Moreover, supervised fine-tuning (SFT) or reinforcement learning (RL) alone cannot dynamically address weaknesses exposed during training. We propose COAL-SQL, a unified framework combining Coverage-Guided Augmentation (CGA) and Failure-Driven Learning (FDL). CGA uses greedy selection to identify SQL structures missing from the original dataset and constructs complementary examples, improving structural coverage. FDL retains GRPO as the main optimization objective while supplying targeted supervision for unsolved examples. At the step level, it applies SFT to verified reasoning traces generated by a strong LLM for accumulated failures. At the epoch level, it retrieves structurally related examples based on accumulated failures to create targeted practice, helping the model acquire the corresponding SQL capabilities. With only 12,600 distinct post-training examples, COAL-SQL achieves 64.9% execution accuracy on the BIRD development set and outperforms baselines trained at comparable scale. The code is available at https://github.com/TechNomad-ds/COAL-SQL.

</details>


### [91] [VISPATH: Visual-Intent-Guided Path Reasoning for Multimodal Knowledge Graph Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20843)
*Jinke Wu,Zhengpin Li,Mengzhe Jia,Yang Li,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出VISPATH框架，通过视觉意图引导的多跳路径推理解决多模态知识图谱问答中推理退化为纯文本图搜索的问题，并构建VISPATH-Bench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态KGQA方法仅在起始实体定位或证据检索阶段使用多模态信息，后续多跳推理退化为纯文本图搜索，无法利用中间跳处才变得重要的多模态线索。

Method: VISPATH包含四个环节：(1)结合多模态grounding与图结构线索识别可靠起始实体；(2)意图引导的路径发现，基于输入、问题与当前部分路径重算每跳的多模态意图，使每次扩展由当前推理状态引导；(3)推理链剪枝，将候选路径作为完整证据链，依据其与问题、推理草图及跳特定意图的一致性进行评估；(4)验证所选证据是否足以生成答案。

Result: 在VISPATH-Bench及三个额外多模态QA基准上，VISPATH持续优于强基线；以GPT-4o为骨干时在VISPATH-Bench上超过GPT-5.4，平均准确率相对提升10.6%，2跳推理提升13.1%。

Conclusion: VISPATH通过跳特定的多模态意图重算与推理链剪枝，将多模态线索贯穿多跳推理全过程，验证了多模态意图引导路径推理的有效性；VISPATH-Bench覆盖2至4跳KG路径问题，为该方向提供评测基准。

Abstract: Knowledge graph question answering (KGQA) enables models to answer natural-language questions through structured graph reasoning and has achieved substantial progress across many benchmarks and applications. Recently, multimodal KGQA (MM-KGQA) has attracted increasing attention because many questions require jointly using multimodal inputs and KG evidence. However, existing MM-KGQA methods typically use multimodal information only for starting entity grounding or evidence retrieval, after which multi-hop reasoning degenerates into text-only graph search. As a result, they cannot exploit multimodal cues that become important at intermediate hops. To address this limitation, we propose VISPATH, a visual-intent-guided path reasoning framework for MM-KGQA. VISPATH first identifies a reliable starting entity by combining multimodal grounding with graph-structural cues. It then performs intent-guided path discovery by recomputing hop-specific multimodal intent from the input, question, and current partial paths, so that each expansion is guided by the current reasoning state. The discovered paths are further refined through reasoning-chain pruning, which evaluates candidate paths as complete evidence chains based on their consistency with the question, reasoning sketch, and hop-specific intent. Finally, VISPATH checks whether the selected evidence is sufficient for answer generation. We further construct VISPATH-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating multimodal multi-hop reasoning over KGs, covering questions that require two to four hops over KG paths. Extensive experiments on VISPATH-Bench and three additional multimodal QA benchmarks show that VISPATH consistently outperforms strong baselines. Notably, with GPT-4o as the backbone, VISPATH surpasses GPT-5.4 on VISPATH-Bench, achieving a 10.6% relative improvement in average accuracy and a 13.1% improvement at 2-hop reasoning.

</details>


### [92] [Boosting Deepresearch and LongContext Ability with Self-Generated Deepresearch Rollouts Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20844)
*Zihan Wang,Hao Wang,Boyuan Jiang,Yiqun Zhang,Shi Feng,Xiaocui Yang,Yiwen Ye,Jianghang Lin,Xiaozhong Ji,Jinghao Lin,Kai Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出 DR-to-Long 数据构造方法与 DLD-RL 三阶段训练框架，通过将深度研究智能体的轨迹转化为长上下文问答数据，显著提升模型的长上下文理解与深度研究能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度研究智能体在多轮搜索与网页访问中上下文快速膨胀，即使经过 DR-RL 训练，仍有 61.6% 的预测错误源于长上下文理解不足（如长上下文幻觉、跨文档证据整合失败）。因此需要通过强化长上下文能力来突破 DR-RL 的性能瓶颈。但长上下文训练不仅需要增加长度，还面临训练数据匮乏的问题。

Method: 提出 DR-to-Long 方法：复用 DR-RL 轨迹（天然包含搜索历史、访问网页、证据片段与最终答案监督），将轨迹中的紧凑片段和网页摘要替换为对应 URL 的完整内容，从而在保持原始证据关系的同时生成更长的多文档上下文，零标注成本。在此基础上提出 DLD-RL 三阶段训练：先进行短程 DR-RL 收集轨迹，再转换为 LongQA 实例并进行 LongQA-RL 以增强长上下文能力，最后进行完整 DR-RL 继续提升深度研究能力。

Result: 在三个 Deepresearch 基准上，DLD-RL 相比标准 DR-RL 提升 7.3%；在三个长上下文基准上提升 13.5%。

Conclusion: 长上下文理解是 DR-RL 的关键瓶颈，通过将智能体轨迹转化为长上下文问答数据并采用分阶段强化学习，可同时增强长上下文与深度研究能力，且无需额外标注成本。

Abstract: Deepresearch (DR) agents interact with real-world web environments through multi-turn search and visit, causing their contexts to grow rapidly over time. We observe that, even after DR Agentic Reinforcement Learning (DR-RL), 61.6% of the model's remaining prediction errors can still be attributed to insufficient long-context understanding, including longcontext hallucination and failures in cross-document evidence integration. It motivates us to further break the bottleneck of DR-RL by strengthening the model's long-context ability. However, effective LongContext training requires more than simply increasing context length. To bridge the data gap, we propose `DR Rollouts to LongContext-QA (DR-to-Long)'. The method repurposes DR-RL trajectories, which naturally contain search histories, visited webpages, evidence snippets, and final-answer supervision. It then replaces the compact snippets and webpage summaries in each trajectory with the full contents of their corresponding URLs, producing substantially longer multi-document contexts while preserving the original evidence relationships. Building on DR-to-Long, we introduce DLD (DR -> LongQA -> DR)-RL. DLD-RL first performs a short DR-RL stage to collect rollout trajectories, which are then converted into LongQA instances at zero annotation cost. The model is subsequently optimized with LongQA-RL to strengthen LongContext ability, followed by full DR-RL to continue improving its DR capability. Experiments show that DLD-RL outperforms standard DR-RL by 7.3% on three Deepresearch benchmarks and improves performance by 13.5% on three long-context benchmarks.

</details>


### [93] [Reading Less While Writing: A Closed-Form Bandwidth Dial for Streaming Multimodal Decoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20845)
*Yasir Mehmood,Kashif Javed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 ZENDAYA，一种用单一连续参数 γ 控制的流式解码调度，将可见源前缀定义为生成进度的闭式函数，统一了离线与流式解码，并证明结构依赖定理，在多种任务上以更少读取量匹配或超越固定调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统解码器需消费完整输入才输出词，离线可行但实时流式不可行；现有流式方案采用固定规则（如 wait-k），不论输入长度或节奏都为每个词等待相同数量的输入 token，缺乏灵活性与可解释性。作者希望用统一、可控的调度替代固定偏移，同时保证流式解码的正确性（不依赖尚未到达的输入）。

Method: 用单连续参数 γ 取代固定偏移，构建 ZENDAYA 调度：可见源前缀为生成进度的闭式函数，并依据输入自身预测长度进行缩放，使离线解码器与实时流式解码器成为同一家族的两个端点。该标量以闭式确定每个输出词平均消耗的源比例：E(γ) ≈ 1/(1+γ)，兼具延迟调节与可解释预算功能。理论方面给出结构依赖定理：在任何预先设定且非递减的调度下，已输出的 token 不可能依赖于尚未到达的输入，该保证对训练与未训练权重均成立，并推广至任意异步到达的无界流。

Result: 实验结论反直觉：读取更少源信息反而可能生成更好文本，因为过多源内容会在模型自身输出锚点最少时稀释注意力。在两种模态与三个公开语料（Charades-STA、ActivityNet Captions、LibriHeavy）上从头训练的紧凑 29M 参数解码器，在读取更少源的情况下匹配或超越固定调度，且在最低延迟时增益最显著（固定偏移此时会崩溃）。流式 METEOR 指标在三个语料上均获得统计显著提升。

Conclusion: ZENDAYA 以单一参数统一离线与流式解码，提供闭式可解释的延迟预算，并有非依赖未来输入的理论保证；在多个模态与语料上验证了“少看可更好”的反直觉现象，低延迟场景优势尤其明显，为该方向提供了紧凑高效的解码方案。

Abstract: A decoder that turns video or audio into text conventionally consumes the entire input before emitting a word. Offline this is merely more than the task requires; live it is impossible, since a caption cannot wait for a match to end. Streaming systems bolt on a fixed rule such as wait-$k$, which waits for the same number of input tokens before every word, regardless of the input's length or pace.
  We replace the fixed offset with ZENDAYA, a schedule governed by a single continuous parameter $γ$. It makes the visible source prefix a closed-form function of generation progress, scaled by the input's own predicted length, so an ordinary offline decoder and a real-time streaming decoder become two endpoints of one family rather than separate models. The same scalar fixes, in closed form, the mean fraction of source consumed per emitted word, $\bar{E}(γ) \approx 1/(1+γ)$, making it at once a latency dial and an interpretable budget. We prove a structural dependency theorem: under any schedule fixed in advance and non-decreasing, no emitted token can depend on input that has not yet arrived. The guarantee holds for trained and untrained weights alike, and extends to unbounded streams under arbitrary asynchronous arrival.
  The empirical result is counterintuitive: seeing less can produce better text, because a flood of source dilutes attention exactly when the model has the least of its own output to anchor on. Trained from scratch across two modalities and three public corpora (Charades-STA, ActivityNet Captions, LibriHeavy), a compact 29M-parameter decoder matches or beats the fixed schedule while reading less of the source, with the sharpest gains at the lowest latencies, where a fixed offset collapses. Streaming METEOR gains are statistically significant on all three corpora.

</details>


### [94] [Rewarding Efficient Reasoning Improves Abstention on Underspecified Tasks in Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20846)
*Polina Tsvilodub,Max Höth,Michael Franke,Björn Deiseroth,Carina Kauf*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 现代大型推理模型（LRMs）在给出正确答案上表现优异，但难以判断何时应放弃作答。本文通过人类研究对比指出：人类在不可作答任务上的推理投入低于可作答任务，而LRM却相反，在不可作答提示上生成更长的思维链、浪费算力。受人类认知资源理性视角启发，作者提出一种新的GRPO奖励，鼓励模型高效判断任务是否包含求解所需全部信息。微调多个4B LRM后，放弃作答能力平均提升12.8%，同时保持作答能力并使思维链平均缩短44%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LRM虽能给出正确答案，却缺乏“知止”能力——不知道何时应拒绝作答。作者希望揭示这一能力缺口，并从人类认知的资源理性角度寻找改进思路。

Method: 将LRM行为与人类研究结果对比，分析可作答与不可作答任务上的推理投入差异；受人类认知资源理性视角启发，设计一种新的GRPO奖励，激励模型高效判断任务是否包含求解所需的全部信息；用该奖励微调多个4B LRM。

Result: 人类在不可作答任务上的推理投入以可作答任务为上界；LRM则相反，在不可作答提示上生成更长的思维链，浪费计算资源。用新奖励微调后，多个4B LRM在类人放弃作答表现上平均提升12.8%，作答能力得以保留，且思维链平均缩短44%。

Conclusion: LRM存在“不知何时放弃作答”的关键能力缺口，且其推理资源分配与人类资源理性模式相悖。通过引入面向“信息充分性判断”的GRPO奖励，可在不损害作答能力的前提下显著提升放弃作答表现并提高推理效率，为构建更高效、更类人的推理模型提供了可行路径。

Abstract: While modern large reasoning models (LRMs) excel at providing correct answers in many tasks, we provide additional evidence for the observation that they often struggle with a critical capability: knowing when to abstain from answering. We analyze this gap by comparing LRM behavior to results from a human study, revealing that human reasoning effort on unanswerable tasks is upper-bounded by answerable tasks, whereas LRMs waste computational resources by generating longer Chains of Thought (CoTs) on unanswerable than on answerable prompts. To overcome this inefficiency, we take inspiration from a resource-rational perspective on human cognition and introduce a novel GRPO reward that encourages efficient reasoning about whether the task contains all the information needed to solve it. Fine-tuning several 4B LRMs with this reward leads to human-like abstention performance gains (+12.8% on average) while retaining answering capabilities and boosting the models' efficiency (44% shorter CoTs on average).

</details>


### [95] [Reading Anxiety or Reading the Label? Comparing Fine-Tuned and Frontier Models for Anxiety Detection on Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20847)
*Cris Huynh,Arlene Pham*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文在受控协议下比较六种焦虑检测方案（前沿商用模型、本地微调模型、传统分类器），并揭示评估中的关键词混杂：69.3%的焦虑标签帖子含"anxiety"或其变体，导致分类器可靠关键词匹配虚高得分。删除相关词后重评发现词依赖度在8.6–25.4点间，且与模型能力无关；已发表指标因此属上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 焦虑是最常见心理健康问题之一，用户常在寻求临床帮助前先在网上书写。构建检测工具的实践者面临具体选择：调用前沿商用模型、本地微调较小模型，还是部署传统分类器。同时，当前该任务的评估方式可能存在混杂，亟需澄清不同方案的性能与评估可靠性。

Method: 在单一受控协议下，于留出的Reddit测试集上比较六种条件，覆盖前沿商用模型、本地微调模型与传统分类器三类方案。识别出评估混杂：69.3%的焦虑标签帖子含"anxiety"或其变体，约为其他可比条件的两倍，使分类器可依赖关键词匹配而非建模疾病语言。因此对每个模型评估两次：原文与删除这些词后的文本，将差值定义为词依赖度（lexical dependence）。

Result: 前沿模型在焦虑F1上领先（0.846），但一个1.1亿参数的领域自适应编码器达到0.831，且不依赖外部API；心理健康领域预训练仅贡献其中0.7分。词依赖度跨度为8.6至25.4点，且不与模型能力对应：LoRA微调的3B模型是测试中最依赖关键词的条件，甚至高于TF-IDF分类器；前沿零样本模型依赖度最低。

Conclusion: 该语料上的已发表数字应视为上界，且这种膨胀对微调模型最大——而此类模型正是已发表结果通常报告的。评估应考虑删除关键词后重测以衡量词依赖度，避免将关键词匹配误判为语言建模能力。

Abstract: Anxiety is among the most common mental health conditions, and people often write about it online well before seeking clinical help. Practitioners building detection tools face a concrete choice: call a frontier commercial model, fine-tune a smaller model in-house, or deploy a conventional classifier. We compare six conditions spanning all three on a held-out Reddit test set under a single controlled protocol. We also identify a confound in how this task is evaluated. In the corpus used here, 69.3% of anxiety-labelled posts contain the word "anxiety" or a variant, roughly twice the rate of comparable conditions, so a classifier can score well by keyword matching rather than by modelling the language of the condition. We therefore evaluate every model twice, on original text and with those terms deleted, and report the difference as lexical dependence. A frontier model leads on anxiety F1 (0.846), but a 110M-parameter domain-adapted encoder reaches 0.831 with no external API dependency, and mental-health domain pretraining accounts for only 0.7 of those points. Lexical dependence spans 8.6 to 25.4 points and does not track model capability: the LoRA fine-tuned 3B model is the most keyword-dependent condition tested, above even a TF-IDF classifier, while the frontier zero-shot model is the least. Published figures on this corpus are therefore upper bounds, and the inflation is largest for the fine-tuned models such figures typically report.

</details>


### [96] [Enhancing Audio Reasoning via Semantic Summary Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20849)
*Francesco Bonzi,Pooneh Mousavi,Cem Subakan,Mirco Ravanelli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SPARE方法，通过在推理开始前用register token锚定目标语义，缓解LALM推理间隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型音频语言模型（LALMs）在复杂问答上表现良好，但存在"推理间隙"：显式思维链（CoT）反而比直接回答精度更低。作者假设长推理序列会将注意力从音频输入上转移走。

Method: SPARE（Semantic Prediction for Audio REasoning）：引入一个register token，通过余弦相似度损失与最终结论的Sentence-BERT嵌入对齐，从而在推理开始前用目标语义目标条件化模型的潜空间。

Result: 在MMAU和MMAR基准上，基于SALMONN的实验表明零样本推理能力提升，且对音频的早期注意力更强，同时不增加推理成本。

Conclusion: 通过在推理前锚定语义目标，可有效缓解LALM的推理间隙，提升零样本音频推理性能与音频注意力，且无需额外推理开销。

Abstract: Large Audio Language Models (LALMs) perform well on complex question answering but often show a reasoning gap, where explicit Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reduces accuracy compared to direct answers. We hypothesize that long reasoning sequences shift attention away from the audio input. To address this, we propose SPARE (Semantic Prediction for Audio REasoning), which introduces a register token aligned with the final conclusion using a cosine similarity loss with a Sentence-BERT embedding. This conditions the model's latent space with the target semantic goal before reasoning begins. Experiments on MMAU and MMAR with SALMONN show improved zero-shot reasoning and stronger early attention to audio without additional inference cost.

</details>


### [97] [MME-Safety: A Fine-grained Benchmark for Safety Evaluation of MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20850)
*Yueming Lyu,Yilian Shi,Haoxiang Tan,Linzhuang Zou,Qihao Wang,Guihua Yu,Jie Qin,Xin Gao,Chenyang Si,Jing Dong,Caifeng Shan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出 MME-Safety 基准，构建四维标注体系与分层评估框架，对17个先进多模态大模型进行零样本安全评估，揭示跨模态输入配置与思维链推理的安全隐患。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型（MLLMs）的跨模态能力引入复杂脆弱性，易绕过单模态过滤器；现有基准缺乏细粒度意图相关标注且依赖单一维度指标，难以全面评估鲁棒性。

Method: 提出 MME-Safety，一个经严格验证的基准，采用独特的四维标注体系对风险场景、危害严重程度及模态特定隐蔽水平进行分类；并引入分层评估框架，评估基础响应可靠性、实际风险暴露以及防御行为的结构完整性。

Result: 对17个最先进 MLLMs 进行广泛零样本评估，提供了当前多模态系统的全面安全画像；系统性地探究了跨模态输入配置，并揭示与思维链（CoT）推理相关的安全影响。

Conclusion: 多维发现凸显了在多模态领域中对鲁棒且具备推理感知能力的安全对齐的迫切需求。

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) show remarkable advancements, their cross-modal capabilities introduce complex vulnerabilities that easily bypass unimodal filters. Existing benchmarks lack fine-grained intent-related annotations and rely on unidimensional metrics, hindering comprehensive robustness evaluation. To address this, we propose MME-Safety, a rigorously verified benchmark featuring a unique four-dimensional annotation schema that categorizes risk scenarios, harm severity, and modality-specific stealth levels. Furthermore, we introduce a hierarchical evaluation framework to assess fundamental response reliability, actual risk exposure, and the structural integrity of defensive behaviors. Extensive zero-shot evaluations across 17 state-of-the-art MLLMs provide a comprehensive safety profile of current multimodal systems. Our analysis systematically investigates cross-modal input configurations and uncovers safety implications associated with Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning. These multifaceted findings underscore the urgent need for robust, reasoning-aware safety alignment in the multimodal landscape.

</details>


### [98] [Generative Artificial Intelligence Chatbots for Motivational Interviewing: A Scoping Review From System Design to Intervention Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20902)
*Runze Hu,Jingqi Kong,Yang Yang,Yihang Yang,Jingyao Liu,Haizhou Tang,Shanghang Zhang,Zheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该范围综述（PRISMA-ScR）纳入47篇报告（48项研究），系统刻画了生成式AI驱动动机性访谈（GenAI-MI）聊天机器人在系统设计、安全性、MI质量、用户感知及干预结局方面的证据，发现其可提供MI一致性的互动且用户评价积极，但持续行为与功能改变证据有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机性访谈（MI）是激发健康行为改变自主动机的协作式方法，生成式AI为通过对话系统实施MI提供了新途径，但相关系统的设计、评估及向干预转化的证据高度碎片化，亟需系统梳理。

Method: 采用PRISMA-ScR范围综述方法，检索9个数据库，纳入2015年1月1日至2026年6月2日期间发表或公开的、使用GenAI生成MI聊天机器人回应或咨询师话语的研究；依据预设框架提取数据并进行描述性综合。

Result: 共纳入47篇报告（48项研究）。20项（41.7%）聚焦系统设计而无直接参与者使用，28项（58.3%）涉及直接互动；多数系统为文本型、无具身化，23项（47.9%）具备动态适应能力。安全措施报告不均衡：直接使用类研究中21/28（75.0%）报告了知情同意或用户教育。30项（62.5%）评估了MI质量，总体提示互动符合MI一致性。用户感知积极，尤其体现在共情、可用性、有用性和使用意愿，但测量工具异质性大。18项（37.5%）报告干预结局，多为单次会话后评估；短期动机方面阳性结果较一致，而持续性行为或功能改变的证据有限。

Conclusion: GenAI-MI聊天机器人能够提供MI一致性的互动并获得积极用户感知，但持续性行为或功能改变的证据有限。未来研究应加强运行时的安全监测、标准化MI质量评估，并采用含行为与功能结局的长期对照设计。

Abstract: Motivational interviewing (MI) is a collaborative approach to elicit autonomous motivation for health behavior change. Generative AI (GenAI) offers new ways to deliver MI via conversational systems, but evidence on their design, assessment, and translation into interventions remains fragmented. This scoping review characterized evidence on GenAI-MI chatbots across system design, safety, MI quality, user perceptions, and intervention outcomes. We conducted a PRISMA-ScR scoping review. Nine datasets were searched for studies published or publicly available from January 1, 2015 to June 2, 2026 that used GenAI to generate MI chatbot responses or counselor utterances. Data were extracted using a predefined framework and synthesized descriptively. Forty-seven reports (48 studies) were included. Twenty (41.7%) focused on system design without direct participant use; 28 (58.3%) involved direct interaction. Most systems were text based and disembodied; 23 (47.9%) incorporated dynamic adaptation. Safety measures were unevenly reported. Among studies with direct use, 21/28 (75.0%) reported informed consent or user education. Thirty (62.5%) assessed MI quality, generally suggesting MI-consistent interactions. User perceptions were favorable, especially empathy, usability, helpfulness, and intention to use, though measures were heterogeneous. Eighteen (37.5%) reported intervention outcomes, mostly after a single session. Positive findings were more consistent for short-term motivation than sustained behavioral or functional change. GenAI-MI chatbots can deliver MI-consistent interactions perceived favorably, but evidence for sustained behavioral or functional change is limited. Future research should strengthen runtime safety monitoring, standardize MI quality assessment, and use longer-term comparative designs with behavioral and functional outcomes.

</details>


### [99] [$μ^2$-Bench: A Multilingual Machine Unlearning Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20945)
*Kyomin Hwang,Hyeonjin Kim,Hyunho Lee,Yearim Kim,Yeji Song,Nojun Kwak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出μ²-Bench，一个针对多语言机器遗忘（MMU）的评测基准，模拟记忆、遗忘与评估全流程，覆盖多语言并兼顾训练语言与留出语言，揭示成功MMU需适配多语言特性的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言大模型中有害内容、隐私数据等不良信息会通过直接训练和跨语言间接传播；现有MMU评估不足，无法判断遗忘是否在所有语言中真正消除了目标知识，因此需要专门的评测基准来填补空白。

Method: 构建μ²-Bench基准，模拟记忆→遗忘→评估的完整流程；其设计特点为：1）覆盖广泛语言集合；2）同时在训练语言与留出语言上评估；3）将知识视为分散于多语言中进行评估；并基于该基准开展分析以提炼MMU洞见。

Result: 研究表明，成功的多语言机器遗忘需要采用能够反映多语言特性的方法，而非简单沿用单语言遗忘策略；基准分析进一步提供了对MMU的更深入理解。

Conclusion: μ²-Bench为多语言机器遗忘提供了系统化评测手段，强调跨语言评估与知识分散性，并为设计符合多语言特性的遗忘方法提供依据与洞察。

Abstract: Undesired information such as harmful content and private data propagates through Multilingual Large Language Models (LLMs) via direct training and indirect cross-linguistic spread. Multilingual Machine Unlearning (MMU) aims to remove such information, yet its evaluation remains underexplored, leaving unclear whether unlearning truly eliminates target knowledge across all languages. To bridge this gap, we introduce $μ^2$-Bench, an MMU benchmark that simulates the full pipeline of memorization, unlearning, and evaluation across diverse languages. It 1) spans a broad set of languages, 2) evaluates on both training and hold-out languages, and 3) assesses knowledge as dispersed across multiple languages. We show that successful MMU requires methods that reflect multilingual characteristics, and conduct analysis to provide deeper insights into MMU.

</details>


### [100] [Aligning with Lived Experience: Heterogeneous Benefits of Fine Tuning in Mental Health Support Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21075)
*Mohit Chandra,Nabin Kim,Eli Min,Aamogh Sawant,Tanmay Sutar,Munmun De Choudhury*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了COPES数据集与三维评估框架，用于评测LLM生成的心理健康同伴支持回复与社区视角的对齐程度，发现后训练可显著提升对齐但存在异质性与分布偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业心理健康服务可及性有限，许多人转向Reddit等在线平台寻求基于真实生活经验的同伴支持；然而相当比例的求助帖无人回应，为利用LLM填补这一空缺提供了机会。尽管LLM在临床基准上表现优异，但其生成"基于生活经验、契合社区规范"的同伴支持的能力仍缺乏探究。

Method: 提出社区中心的同伴互助支持数据集（COPES），并设计三维评估框架，从社区视角衡量LLM对心理健康求助类问题的对齐程度；对零样本模型以及经SFT和DPO后训练的模型进行评估，并分析不同子社区与应对策略下的对齐差异。

Result: 在COPES上进行后训练可使通用模型的策略对齐提升超过50%，并改善情绪与语气维度的对齐；但提升具有异质性，在不同subreddit和所需应对策略间差异显著。此外，后训练引发分布偏移，明显偏向问题聚焦型建议，同时抑制情绪聚焦型策略。

Conclusion: 基于社区驱动数据构建数据集可提升LLM回复的对齐程度，但模型在不同子社区和具体心理健康需求上的表现仍不均衡，需进一步关注社区多样性与需求覆盖。

Abstract: As access to professional mental healthcare remains limited, many individuals turn to online platforms such as Reddit to seek peer support situated within human lived experience. However, a significant portion of such queries go unanswered, presenting an opportunity for using Large Language Models (LLMs) to fill this gap. While LLMs have demonstrated strong performance on clinical benchmarks, their ability to generate lived-experience informed and community-aligned peer support is underexplored. Addressing this gap, we introduce the COmmunity-centered Peer Engaged Support (COPES) dataset and a three-axis evaluation framework to assess LLM alignment with community perspectives to mental health support seeking queries. Evaluating zero-shot and post-trained (SFT and DPO) models, we show that post-training on COPES significantly improves Strategy Alignment (>50% for general-purpose models) and alignment in Emotion & Tone. However, we also observe that such improvements are heterogeneous and alignment improvements vary significantly across subreddits and requested coping strategies. Furthermore, post-training induces distributional shifts, heavily favoring problem-focused recommendations while suppressing emotion-focused strategies. Together, this work shows that while curating community-driven data improves the alignment of LLM responses, model performance remains disparate across distinct sub-communities and specific mental health needs.

</details>


### [101] [Geometry of Values: Task Vector Composition for Ethical Preference Alignment in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21094)
*Utkarsh Agarwal,Monojit Choudhury*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文构建了包含12,000条双选项道德困境实例、覆盖三组价值冲突并翻译为五种语言的数据集，用以考察大语言模型的价值观偏好与跨语言行为；实验发现GPT-5-mini在无政策提示下稳定偏好诚实高于自主，Llama-3.2-1/3B存在首位选项偏差且可通过微调或DPO消除，并借助任务向量迁移与正交化方法分离出特定价值偏好的方向，实现立场反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型被日益部署于需要权衡冲突道德价值的场景，但即便强模型也存在隐藏偏见，且跨语言指令遵循能力脆弱。现有研究缺乏对多语言、多价值对冲突情境下模型价值偏好的系统刻画，亦难以区分模型所学到的究竟是抽象价值取向还是数据集中的统计相关性。因此作者希望构建跨语言道德困境基准，并提出方法将抽象价值偏好方向从一般指令遵循能力中解耦出来。

Method: 构建12,000条双选项道德困境实例，覆盖Honesty vs. Justice、Justice vs. Autonomy、Autonomy vs. Honesty三组两两价值冲突，并翻译为印地语、阿拉伯语、西班牙语和中文，以探测跨语言行为。对GPT-5-mini及Llama-3.2-1B/3B进行基准测试，并分别采用普通微调与直接偏好优化（DPO）微调。此外提出基于任务向量迁移的实验：先计算价值偏好方向的任务向量，再将其对一般指令遵循向量做正交化，以隔离特定价值偏好方向，并通过任务算术获得立场相反的模型。

Result: GPT-5-mini在无政策提示下于五种语言中均一致偏好Honesty高于Autonomy。Llama-3.2-1B/3B表现出强烈的首位选项偏差，而普通微调与DPO微调均能有效消除该偏差，将准确率提升至98%以上。任务向量迁移实验中，将价值偏好方向对一般指令遵循向量正交化后，能够有效隔离特定价值偏好方向，并成功通过任务算术得到立场相反的模型。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个多语言、多价值冲突的道德困境数据集，揭示了强模型与开源小模型在价值偏好与选项位置偏差上的差异，并证明微调与DPO可矫正偏差。更重要的是，任务向量正交化方法能够将抽象价值偏好从一般指令遵循能力中解耦，为可控地调节模型价值立场提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in applications that must weigh clashing moral values, yet even strong models exhibit hidden biases and brittle instruction-following across languages. We introduce a 12,000-instance dataset of two-option dilemmas covering pairwise three value conflicts: Honesty vs. Justice, Justice vs. Autonomy, and Autonomy vs. Honesty, along with their translations into Hindi, Arabic, Spanish, and Chinese, to probe cross-lingual behavior. Benchmarking on GPT-5-mini reveals that it consistently favors Honesty over Autonomy across all five languages when no policy is given. The Llama-3.2-1/3B models exhibit strong first-option bias; however, both plain fine-tuning and Direct Preference Optimization fine-tuning effectively remove this bias, increasing accuracy to greater than 98%. In order to decouple the effect of learning correlations in the dataset from abstract values, we propose a task vector transfer based experiment where after computing the task vectors for a direction of value preference we orthogonalize it with respect to the general instruction following vector. Our experiment shows that this method is effective in isolating the direction of the specific value preference that can successfully be used to conduct task arithmetic to obtain a model with the opposite stance.

</details>


### [102] [From Task Success to Productive Success: Evaluating Human-AI Collaboration by Quality and Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21117)
*Saki Imai,Mert İnan,Malihe Alikhani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出以"产出质量/交互成本"为核心的人机协作生产力评估框架，揭示相同质量下交互成本可相差70倍，且质量-成本关系因任务而异，主观评分不能替代生产力指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生产力评估多用任务完成时间、经济价值或产出质量改进来度量，但这些指标将协作视为黑箱：只关注产出了什么，忽略了产出过程中的交互成本。受经济学文献启发，作者希望将交互成本显性化，从而区分"高成本的成功"与"高生产力的成功"。

Method: 提出以产出质量相对于交互成本为核心的生产力导向评估框架；在涵盖四项任务的两个数据集上，对会话的交互成本与质量评分进行对比分析，并结合对话分析考察高生产力会话的行为特征。

Result: （1）质量评分相同的会话，交互成本最多相差70倍；（2）质量-成本关系因任务而异，部分任务受益于延长交互，另一些则偏好快速收敛；（3）主观用户评分不能可靠替代生产力度量；（4）高生产力会话的特征是智能体更早进行探查（probing），用户用于修复交互的投入更少。

Conclusion: 通过区分高生产力成功与高成本成功，该框架使交互成本变得可见，并表明对话分析可为AI系统的评估与设计提供依据。

Abstract: AI productivity is often measured by task completion time, economic value, or improvements in outcome quality. However, these measures usually treat collaboration as a black box where they capture what output was produced, but not the interaction cost required to produce it. Motivated by economics literature, we introduce a productivity-oriented framework for evaluating human-AI collaboration as outcome quality relative to interaction cost. Across two datasets spanning four tasks, we show that: (1) sessions with identical quality ratings can differ by up to 70 times in interaction cost; (2) quality-cost relationships vary by task, with some tasks rewarding extended interaction and others favoring fast convergence; (3) subjective user ratings are not reliable substitutes for productivity; and (4) productive sessions are characterized by agents probing earlier and users spending less effort repairing the interaction. By distinguishing productive success from costly success, our framework makes interactional cost visible and shows how dialogue analysis can inform the evaluation and design of AI systems.

</details>


### [103] [Scaling Forced Alignment to End-User Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21145)
*Lawry Sorenson,Michael Crandall,Eric K. Ringger,Stephen D. Richardson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文针对语音-文本强制对齐中Viterbi算法二次时空复杂度、难以扩展至长序列的问题，提出结合Hirschberg算法（线性内存）与约束随机游走剪枝（可调置信度、容忍转写错误）两种优化。在三小时输入上，内存从140 GB降至5 MB，CPU下耗时仅为torchaudio的三分之一且对齐结果一致；在超过20分钟的输入上，剪枝带来额外2倍加速，并在98%以上测试案例中保持对齐准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Viterbi算法此前已被用于将音频与文本强制对齐，从而从在线资源中挖掘训练数据，但许多现有实现具有二次时间与空间复杂度，难以扩展到长输入序列。因此需要降低内存占用与计算开销，同时保持对齐质量并容忍转写错误。

Method: 方法包含两项优化：1）采用Hirschberg算法，以线性内存原地完成对齐；2）将语音与文本之间的对齐建模为约束随机游走，从而能够以任意置信度剪枝搜索空间，同时考虑转写错误的影响。

Result: Hirschberg优化在三小时输入上把内存使用从140 GB降至5 MB，在CPU运行时耗时仅为torchaudio的三分之一，且产生完全相同的对齐结果；对于长于20分钟的输入，剪枝带来额外2倍加速，并在超过98%的测试案例中保持对齐准确率。

Conclusion: 通过Hirschberg线性内存对齐与基于约束随机游走的剪枝，可在保证对齐一致性与高准确率的前提下，显著降低长序列强制对齐的内存与时间开销，使其更适合处理长音频输入的训练数据挖掘场景。

Abstract: The Viterbi algorithm has been previously used to perform forced alignment of audio to text to mine training data from online resources. However, many existing implementations have quadratic time and space complexity, scaling poorly to long input sequences. We propose two optimizations to address this issue. First, we apply the Hirschberg algorithm to perform the alignment in place using linear memory. Second, we model the alignment between speech and text as a constrained random walk, allowing us to prune the search space with arbitrary confidence while accounting for transcription errors. The Hirschberg optimization reduces memory usage from 140 GB to 5 MB for three-hour inputs while producing identical alignments in one-third the time of torchaudio when both run on a CPU. We achieve an additional 2x speedup with pruning on inputs longer than 20 minutes while preserving alignment accuracy in more than 98% of tested cases.

</details>


### [104] [CoLearn: An Agentic Tutor that Learns its Learner in a Human--AI Co-Learning Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21154)
*Kailai He,Zhihao Wu,Linhai Zhang,Runcong Zhao,Yulan He,Jiazheng Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CoLearn：一个具备持久学习者状态记忆的自适应智能辅导系统，通过软证据贝叶斯知识追踪与LLM观测函数实现个性化出题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有已部署的辅导工具多采用固定题库，且将错误答案仅视为单一比特信号，无法像优秀人类辅导那样追踪学习者知识状态、诊断错误原因并生成最有助于学习的下一题。

Method: 提出CoLearn，包含三个组件：(i) 持久学习者状态记忆，用贝叶斯知识追踪的软证据变体按主题更新掌握度，由大语言模型充当连续观测函数；(ii) 自适应题目生成，针对学习者最薄弱主题与反复出现的错误概念；(iii) 证据视图，通过实时进度可视化与盲测A/B对比使个性化可见、可验证。

Result: 在盲测A/B评估中，基于该记忆条件生成的题目相较非个性化题目有68–69%的偏好率；在隐藏真实掌握度的角色模拟中，智能体的信念收敛于学习者的真实掌握水平。

Conclusion: CoLearn表明，基于持久、证据驱动的学习者状态记忆进行自适应出题，可显著提升个性化辅导质量，并使其效果可观测、可检验。

Abstract: Good tutoring adapts to the individual: it tracks what a learner knows, notices why they go wrong, and asks the next question that will help most. Most deployed tutoring tools instead serve fixed item banks and treat a wrong answer as a single bit of signal. We present CoLearn, an interactive, agentic tutor that supports an iterative tutoring loop: the learner practises, and the system builds an evidence-grounded memory of the learner's mastery and misconceptions. This memory is updated as evidence accumulates and is used to generate the next personalised question. CoLearn has three components: (i) a persistent learner-state memory that updates per-topic mastery with a soft-evidence variant of Bayesian Knowledge Tracing, where a large language model acts as a continuous observation function; (ii) adaptive question generation that targets the learner's weakest topic and recurring misconceptions; and (iii) an evidence view that makes personalisation visible and testable through live progress visualisation and blind A/B comparison. In blind A/B evaluation, questions conditioned on this memory are preferred over non-personalised ones 68-69% of the time, and in persona simulations with hidden ground-truth mastery the agent's belief converges toward the learner's true mastery.

</details>


### [105] [Not All Irregularity Is Equal: Causally Isolating a Rare Failure Mode in Japanese Morphological Inflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21179)
*Wen Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文对日语过去时动词活用进行正字法感知的诊断，发现字符级 Transformer 虽聚合准确率超 97%，但误差高度集中于占比不足 1% 的特定不规则子类，并通过消融实验证明该子类对误差的贡献远超"不规则性"本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经形态生成系统在基准数据集上的高聚合准确率可能掩盖集中于罕见形态子类的系统性错误，因此需要更细粒度的诊断视角；同时作者主张不应仅把平假名视为转写媒介，而应视为编码形态音系结构的表征系统。

Method: 采用两种字符级 Transformer 架构，在五个随机种子下评估日语过去时动词屈折；随后进行正字法感知的子类诊断，并开展受控消融实验，比较单独移除该 /e/ 结尾、需辅音重叠（gemination）的不规则子类与移除全部不规则动词的准确率变化。

Result: 两种系统聚合准确率均超过 97%，但该占比不足 1% 的不规则子类占据残余错误的 30-43%，其对总错误的贡献约为其流行度的 34-48 倍；消融实验显示，仅移除该子类带来的准确率提升大于移除全部不规则动词之和。

Conclusion: 神经形态学习中的误差集中并非由"不规则性"本身驱动，而是极端低频形态模式与特定正字法过程交互的结果。作者主张形态学评估应纳入细粒度子类分析，并讨论了该结论对数据高效、符合发展规律的语言模型预训练的启示。

Abstract: Neural morphological generation systems often achieve high aggregate accuracy on benchmark datasets, yet such performance can conceal systematic errors clustered in rare morphological subclasses. We present an orthography-aware diagnosis of Japanese past-tense verb inflection, treating hiragana not merely as a transcriptional medium but as a representational system that encodes morphophonological structure. Using two character-level Transformer architectures evaluated across five random seeds, we show that although both systems exceed 97% aggregate accuracy, a single structurally specific irregular subtype, verbs whose stems end in /e/ and require gemination before the past-tense suffix and make up fewer than 1% of the data, accounts for a disproportionate 30-43% share of residual errors and contributes roughly 34-48x its prevalence to total errors. We then move from diagnosis to causal isolation: controlled ablation experiments show that removing this subtype alone produces larger accuracy gains than removing all irregular verbs combined. These findings indicate that error concentration in neural morphological learning is not driven by irregularity per se, but by the interaction between extreme low-frequency morphological patterns and specific orthographic processes. We argue that morphological evaluation should incorporate fine-grained subclass analysis, and discuss implications for data-efficient, developmentally plausible language model pretraining.

</details>


### [106] [When Better Turns Do Not Make Better Agents: Diagnosing the Gap Between Next-Turn Metrics and Workflow Success](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21187)
*Md Tahmid Rahman Laskar,Xue-Yong Fu,Gundeep Singh,Karol Chang,Kevin Sanders,Shi Zong,Tania Habib,Julien Bouvier Tremblay,Shayna Gardiner,Harsh Saini,Matthias Lee,Elena Khasanova,Quinten McNamara,Shashi Bhushan TN*

Main category: cs.CL
